Conclusion: 识别出证据选择、跨域整合和隐私控制为个性化LLM的关键挑战，为未来研究提供明确方向。

Abstract: Existing personalized LLM benchmarks primarily rely on textual personas or isolated behavioral signals, providing limited evaluation of cross-domain behavioral personalization, where responses must be grounded in heterogeneous daily-life activities. To address this gap, we introduce LUNAR, the first benchmark for evaluating how LLMs personalize responses from longitudinal app interaction histories across universal daily-life domains, including clothing, food, housing, and mobility. To support scalable benchmark construction while mitigating data sparsity and privacy concerns, LUNAR uses a multi-stage coarse-to-fine synthesis pipeline grounded in real-world behavioral patterns. Fidelity analyses show closer alignment with real behavioral distributions than other synthetic benchmarks. Experiments on 19 mainstream LLMs show that access to behavioral logs is necessary but not sufficient for deep personalization: neither more context nor larger models guarantees better performance; effective personalization depends on selecting and integrating relevant evidence across domains. Direct retrieval of fine-grained behavioral records consistently outperforms compressed memory, while stronger personalization can come at the cost of privacy protection. These findings identify evidence selection, cross-domain integration, and privacy control as key challenges for personalized LLMs.

</details>


### [114] [WorldClaw: Agentic 3D Open-World Generation at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05248)
*Chunchao Guo,Jinpeng Li,Yang Li,Zilong Huang*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: WorldClaw是一个完全智能体驱动的从粗到细的开放世界3D场景生成框架，通过规划、地形构建和区域细化生成全局一致、局部丰富且可编辑的大规模场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从开放式文本生成大规模可自由探索的3D世界极具挑战性，现有系统难以同时保证全局空间连贯性、丰富局部内容和适合下游编辑与复用的显式资产。

Method: WorldClaw采用全智能体、从粗到细的流程：规划智能体将文本转换为区域、地形、资产、材质和空间关系的结构化规范；随后构建全局一致的地形基础（语义布局、可复用资产、生成式或程序化材质、区域感知高度场）；对细节需求高的区域生成地形条件化组合、重建可编辑纹理网格并恢复其在 terrain 上的位置；渲染智能体进一步细化地形、物体、外观和接触。

Result: 在多样化的开放世界提示下，WorldClaw生成了具有连贯空间组织、视觉吸引力局部内容和可编辑实例级资产的大规模场景，同时保持一致的全局地形结构。

Conclusion: WorldClaw有效解决了开放世界3D生成中全局与局部、可编辑性与复杂性的平衡问题，为大规模场景生成提供了可行的智能体框架。

Abstract: Generating large-scale, freely explorable 3D worlds from open-ended text remains challenging because a system must jointly maintain global spatial coherence, rich local content, and explicit assets suitable for downstream editing and reuse. We present WorldClaw, a fully agentic, coarse-to-fine framework for open-world 3D scene generation. Planning agents translate a text prompt into a structured specification of regions, terrain, assets, materials, and spatial relations. WorldClaw then builds a globally coherent terrain foundation from semantic layouts, reusable assets, generative or procedural materials, and a region-aware height field. For detail-demanding regions, it generates terrain-conditioned compositions, reconstructs editable textured meshes, and recovers their placement on the terrain; render-based agents further refine terrain, objects, appearance, and contacts. Across diverse open-world prompts, WorldClaw produces large-scale scenes with coherent spatial organization, visually compelling local content, and editable instance-level assets while preserving a consistent global terrain structure.

</details>


### [115] [Agentic self-driving microscopy benchmarks support qualification but do not necessarily generalize to unseen tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05266)
*Nathan S Johnson,Ian Abshire*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本研究构建了用于评估大语言模型代理控制显微镜等科学工具的基准与追踪框架，测试了多种代理架构、LLM和参数配置，发现基准虽能用于资格认证和回归测试，但无法可靠预测代理在新任务上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型代理越来越多地用于控制科学仪器，但相关工程范例尚不成熟，设计选择众多，且研究者不仅要求代理能完成已知任务，还希望其能泛化到新任务，因此需要系统评估不同设计选择的影响及基准预测能力的局限。

Method: 开发了基准和追踪日志框架，系统评估了从单代理到三代理的图拓扑、五种LLM、RAG与上下文参数以及操作约束，记录了105种配置、1,949次运行和49,109次RAG检索，并训练了代理预测性能的替代模型。

Result: 直接对比显示不同配置在延迟、token使用、成本和失败模式上有显著差异，但基于架构和测试结果训练的替代模型无法可靠预测代理在新任务上的表现。

Conclusion: 当前基准适用于资格认证、回归测试、诊断和直接对比，但异构测试套件无法支持任务无关的全局配置模型，提示需要重新思考代理泛化评估方法。

Abstract: Large language model agents are increasingly being developed to control a wide range of scientific characterization tools including microscopes and synchrotron beamlines. Research into agentic control of physical infrastructure is nascent and there are few well-established paradigms for how to engineer an agentic system. There are many choices to make when designing a microscopy agent, including the choice of LLM, the number of agents to use, agent responsibilities and delegation rules, retrieval-augmented generation parameters, and more. When designing and optimizing an agentic microscope controller, researchers not only want to ensure that the agent can correctly perform known tasks but also that the agent can generalize to new tasks that it has not encountered before. In this study, we develop a benchmark and trace-logging framework that reveals a) how different choices of agent architecture impact performance at microscopy tasks and b) the limitations of benchmarks for predicting if a particular agent will perform well on unseen microscopy tasks. The framework was used to evaluate one-, two-, and three-agent graph topologies, five LLMs, RAG and context parameters, and operational constraints across 53 microscopy benchmark tests. In total, 105 agent configurations, 1,949 individual test runs, and 49,109 RAG retrievals were recorded. Direct comparisons showed clear differences in latency, token use, cost, and failure mode between configurations. However, surrogate models trained on agent architecture and test results did not reliably predict an agent's performance on new, unseen tasks. These results show that these benchmarks are useful for qualification, regression testing, diagnosis, and direct comparison, but the current heterogeneous test suite does not support a task-independent global configuration model.

</details>


### [116] [Hybrid Probabilistic Zonotopes for Identifiable and Refinable Predictive Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05454)
*Zhen Zhang,Amr Alanwar*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出混合概率Zonotope（HProbZ）输出头，将离散模式选择、有界系统漂移和不可约随机噪声分别建模为二元、有界和随机生成器，支持闭式似然与单次前向传播的跨步不确定性细化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概率预测头（如高斯混合或单一共形区域）无法同时区分预测中离散模式、有界漂移和随机噪声三种异质不确定性来源，且难以在共享结构下利用观测信息更新未来预测。

Method: HProbZ用zonotope的三种生成器分别编码不确定性，通过卷积获得闭式似然；共享有界生成器使各预测步代数耦合，实现单次前向传播的条件细化；并利用共享结构推导解析逐模式风险与无分布多模态共形集。

Result: 理论上证明三种生成器可从似然中可辨识（至置换），且HProbZ密度不同于任何有限高斯混合；实证表明在代表性基准上优于同编码器混合基线，兼具混合与共形预测器无法同时提供的结构特性。

Conclusion: HProbZ为多源不确定性建模提供了统一、可解释且计算高效的方案，在保持预测性能的同时获得理论辨识性与多模态共形保证，填补了现有方法的空白。

Abstract: Probabilistic prediction heads in neural networks typically output either a Gaussian mixture or a single conformal region. Neither separates the distinct sources of uncertainty often present in real prediction tasks: a discrete choice among modes, bounded systematic drift within the chosen mode, and irreducible stochastic noise. We introduce the Hybrid Probabilistic Zonotope (HProbZ), an output head that represents these three sources as binary, bounded, and stochastic generators of a zonotope, and admits a closed-form likelihood by convolution. Sharing the bounded generator across prediction steps couples future predictions algebraically, so observing one step refines the predictive distribution at every remaining step in a single forward pass. We establish that the three generators are identifiable from the likelihood up to permutation, and that an HProbZ density is representationally distinct from any finite Gaussian mixture. The same shared structure provides analytic per-mode risk and distribution-free multi-modal conformal sets at inference time. Empirical analysis on representative prediction benchmarks supports the effectiveness of the design relative to same-encoder mixture baselines, while offering structural properties that mixture or convex-conformal predictors do not jointly provide.

</details>


### [117] [CASCADE: An Agentic Regulatory Network Framework for Patient-Data-Validated Downstream Perturbation Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05359)
*Jose A. Bird*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出CASCADE框架，基于ARACNe网络预测基因扰动的转录效应，并通过TCGA患者肿瘤数据验证方向准确性；发现其预测在部分基因上有效，但未能超越现有知识库；同时评估了LLM代理调用MCP工具的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有验证方法仅检查预测基因是否为已知癌症基因（成员关系），忽略了预测方向是否与真实生物学一致；本文希望通过扩增拷贝数作为敲低的剂量代理，在真实患者数据中测试方向准确性。

Method: 使用ARACNe调控网络通过MCP暴露，预测基因扰动后的下游转录方向；以焦点基因拷贝数扩增作为反向敲低的自然实验，对比TCGA和METABRIC中扩增与非扩增肿瘤的表达差异；并用Fisher精确检验与MSigDB基因集比较，同时单独评测LLM代理将自然语言转为真实MCP调用的准确率。

Result: MYC预测在BRCA（90.0%）、COAD（72.0%）、STAD（85.7%）中显著一致，METABRIC复制87.2%；但未超过MSigDB基线。15个额外基因验证显示基因特异性：增殖机器调控子大多有效，谱系转录因子和CCND2失败。LLM代理在35个查询中，小模型精确匹配71.4%，大模型85.7%；歧义查询上两模型均错误默认扰动类型。

Conclusion: CASCADE在特定基因上能准确预测方向，但整体未超越现有公共知识；验证结果强调基因特异性而非普适性；LLM代理的接地能力受规模影响，但存在顽固的歧义失败模式，需要针对性设计改进。

Abstract: CASCADE is an agentic framework that predicts downstream transcriptional effects of gene perturbation from precomputed
  ARACNe regulatory networks, exposed via MCP. Prior work validates such tools by checking whether predicted genes are
  known cancer genes (membership); we instead test whether the predicted direction of change matches reality, using
  focal-gene copy-number amplification as a dosage-based proxy for the inverse of knockdown against real TCGA patient
  tumor data.
  For MYC, CASCADE's predicted knockdown targets show strong concordance with real amplified-vs-non-amplified tumor
  expression across three cancer types (BRCA: 90.0%, COAD: 72.0%, STAD: 85.7%; all p<0.0013), well above permutation
  baselines, surviving a PAM50 subtype control and replicating in an independent cohort (METABRIC, 87.2%). Compared
  against curated MSigDB gene-set baselines via Fisher's exact test, CASCADE's accuracy is not shown to exceed existing
  public knowledge of MYC- or E2F-driven biology, though its gene-specific direction-calling clearly outperforms a naive
  uniform guess.
  Extending to fifteen additional genes, validation proves gene-specific rather than universal: proliferation-machinery
  regulators mostly replicate, while lineage-identity transcription factors and one cyclin-D paralog (CCND2)
  consistently fail, a pattern we discuss as a hedged, post-hoc hypothesis.
  We separately benchmark whether an LLM-based agent correctly grounds natural-language requests into CASCADE's real MCP
  tool calls. Across 35 queries, a documented local model reaches 71.4% exact match (85.7% for a larger model); schema
  and gene-alias failures are resolved by scale or server-side correction, but both models confidently default to the
  wrong perturbation type on ambiguous queries, a failure a targeted fix could not resolve because its trigger condition
  never occurs.

</details>


### [118] [Counterfactual Analysis via Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05367)
*Zonghao Yang*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文探讨了GPT-3.5在在线借贷反事实分析中的应用，通过提示工程将R²从1.97%提升至2.84%，接近梯度提升回归的3.48%，并展示了其在生成反事实投资回报率时的逻辑一致性与因果推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反事实分析对决策至关重要，但传统机器学习方法在复杂场景中存在局限。作者希望探索LLM（如GPT-3.5）是否能在在线借贷等实际场景中有效进行反事实预测，以提供更灵活、可解释的决策支持。

Method: 研究分为两步：首先评估GPT-3.5的预测性能并与先进机器学习算法比较，通过提示工程优化其输出；其次，利用GPT生成不同利率方案下的反事实ROI，检验其逻辑和因果推理能力。

Result: 提示工程将GPT的R²从1.97%提高到2.84%，接近梯度提升回归的3.48%；GPT生成的反事实ROI表现出逻辑连贯性和因果一致性。

Conclusion: LLM在在线借贷反事实分析中展现出潜力，可扩展至更广泛的预测和决策场景，但需结合提示工程以提升性能。

Abstract: Counterfactual analysis aims to predict potential outcomes under hypothetical scenarios, offering valuable insights for decision-making. This paper investigates the application of large language models (LLMs), specifically the GPT-3.5 model, for counterfactual analysis. We focus on the online lending context, where the counterfactual return on investment (ROI) is crucial for evaluating different interest rate schemes. We begin by assessing the predictive performance of GPT and comparing it with advanced machine learning algorithms. The results show that prompt engineering can significantly enhance GPT's predictions, with the R-squared increasing from 1.97% to 2.84%, closely approaching the 3.48% achieved by gradient-boosted regression. Subsequently, we utilize GPT to generate counterfactual ROIs under a set of alternative interest rates. GPT exhibits logical coherence and causal reasoning in its responses. The findings underscore the potential of LLMs as effective tools for counterfactual analysis in online lending, suggesting broader applications for LLMs in various predictive and decision-making contexts.

</details>


### [119] [DoctorAgents: an agentic framework to iteratively refine AutoML pipeline for small clinical temporal data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05375)
*Ruilin Wang,Bo-Hong Wang,Elizabeth Kourbatski,Jun Bai,Hegang Chen,Ziyang Song,Gilles Boire,Marie Hudson,Yue Li*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出DoctorAgents，一个基于大语言模型智能体的自动化机器学习框架，通过自然语言反馈和文本梯度下降实现推理驱动的端到端ML流水线优化，在小型临床数据上优于传统AutoML。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床数据规模小、异构且时序复杂，现有AutoML依赖暴力搜索且缺乏推理与记忆，难以高效构建可靠ML流水线。

Method: 使用专门化的LLM智能体分别负责生成、验证和优化，通过文本梯度下降反向传播自然语言反馈，实现针对性更新而非全空间搜索。

Result: 在多种临床任务上，DoctorAgents一致优于已有AutoML基线，并生成更具可解释性的任务特定表示。

Conclusion: 将AutoML从穷举搜索重构为推理驱动的精炼过程，为小规模临床数据上的自动化建模提供了更高效、可解释的解决方案。

Abstract: Clinical machine learning (ML) has the potential to support high-stakes medical decision-making, but reliable deployment is often constrained by scarce, heterogeneous, and temporal complexity. Developing effective ML pipelines for such data remains time-consuming and error-prone, while existing automated machine learning (AutoML) systems only partially address this challenge because they largely rely on brute-force search over predefined spaces and lack explicit reasoning and memory. We therefore reformulate AutoML for small clinical data from exhaustive search to reasoning-driven refinement. We propose DoctorAgents, an agentic AI framework that autonomously constructs and optimizes end-to-end ML pipelines through specialized large language model (LLM) agents for generation, validation, and refinement. DoctorAgents backpropagates natural-language feedback through textual gradient descent to perform targeted updates without exhaustive search. Experiments across diverse clinical tasks show that DoctorAgents consistently outperforms established AutoML baselines while producing more interpretable task-specific representations.

</details>


### [120] [Align-RAG: Alignment Is All You Need for TSFM In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05571)
*Mohammad Asadi,Soheil Hor,Bardiya Akhbari,Jack W. O'Sullivan,Tahoura Nedaee,Layne C. Price,Raviteja Anantha,Euan Ashley,Ehsan Adeli*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: Align-RAG是一种无需训练的检索增强预测方法，通过闭式幅度缩放和整数滞后相位移动对齐检索窗口，使冻结时间序列基础模型能直接利用检索上下文，在多个基准上超越需训练的融合模块。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索增强预测依赖训练融合模块，假设冻结骨干无法自行动态利用检索信息；本文质疑该假设，探索无需学习的对齐方法。

Method: 对检索到的过去-未来窗口应用闭式逐对幅度重缩放和整数滞后相位移动，再输入冻结骨干，全程无学习参数。

Result: 在冻结Chronos-Bolt上平均MSE降低3.75%，并在四个额外冻结TSFM上零样本MSE提升2.5%-13.7%；对齐演示的预测位移匹配闭式岭预测。

Conclusion: 冻结TSFM已具备动态上下文检索能力，闭式对齐应作为检索增强预测的默认基线，无需训练融合模块。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented forecasting promises to adapt frozen Time Series Foundation Models (TSFMs) to new domains without fine-tuning, but recent methods typically rely on learned fusion modules, i.e., trained adapters that merge retrieved examples into the backbone's forecast, based on the assumption that frozen backbones cannot dynamically incorporate retrieved context on their own. We show this assumption is unnecessary. We introduce Align-RAG, a training-free method that applies a closed-form per-pair amplitude rescaling and integer-lag phase shift to retrieved past-future windows before they enter a frozen backbone's context. With no learned parameters, Align-RAG outperforms the state-of-the-art trained retrieval adapter on a frozen Chronos-Bolt on all seven datasets of the standard benchmark (avg -3.75% MSE), showing that the gains previously attributed to learned fusion are recoverable without any training. Align-RAG further improves zero-shot MSE on four additional frozen TSFMs with various architectures by 2.5% to 13.7% per backbone with no per-backbone tuning. To probe why alignment helps, we compare the frozen backbone's prediction shift under aligned demonstrations to the closed-form ridge prediction shift on the same pairs. We find that aligned demonstrations induce prediction shifts that track a closed-form ridge predictor on the same pairs, with a future-shuffle control ruling out a futures-averaging account. Together, these results indicate that frozen TSFMs already support dynamic in-context use of retrievals, and that closed-form alignment should be the default baseline for retrieval-augmented forecasting before any fusion module is trained. Code available at: https://github.com/masadi-99/align-rag

</details>


### [121] [C$^3$PO: Evaluating Cross-Modal Composition and Counterfactual Performance in Omnimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05381)
*Swapnanil Mukherjee,Agyeya Negi,Tanuja Ganu,Ponnurangam Kumaraguru*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出C3PO基准测试，揭示多模态大模型在跨模态推理中因主导模态偏见而失败，注意力机制过早集中于文本导致性能大幅落后于人类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLMs虽能处理多模态输入，但推理仍偏向主导模态，导致跨模态推理脆弱，缺乏系统性诊断工具。

Method: 构建包含3404个样本的C3PO基准，覆盖视频/音频/图像/文本，设计信息组合与反事实冲突两类任务，采用25个逻辑模板自动生成，并通过注意力探针分析失败机制。

Result: 人类准确率88.64%，最佳模型Gemini-3.1-Pro仅73.17%；86-95%失败源于模态主导，注意力87-95%集中于文本；中层注意力熵可预测正确性，模板复杂度差异导致56点准确率差距。

Conclusion: 多模态感知不保证稳健推理，架构需支持持续跨模态注意力以避免过早收敛，当前模型在冲突解决中缺乏结构性的模态角色处理能力。

Abstract: Current Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) can process diverse sensory inputs, yet their reasoning remains heavily biased toward a dominant modality, resulting in brittle cross-modal reasoning. We introduce C$^3$PO, a benchmark of 3,404 samples spanning video, audio, image, and text, evaluating two abilities: information composition (fusing dispersed evidence) and counterfactual conflict (resolving deliberate contradictions). C$^3$PO's paired IC/CC structure and four-tier design enable targeted diagnosis of when and why cross-modal reasoning fails. Built through a fully automatic pipeline using 25 logically grounded templates, C$^3$PO reveals that while humans achieve 88.64% accuracy, the best model (Gemini-3.1-Pro) reaches only 73.17%, with open-source models collapsing under conflict. Through attention probes, we find 86-95% of failures stem from modality dominance: models commit to one modality while ignoring contradictory evidence, concentrating 87-95% of attention on text. Mid-layer attention entropy predicts correctness-sustained exploration succeeds, premature collapse fails. The 56-point accuracy gap between equally complex templates reveals that performance depends on modalities' structural roles in conflict resolution, not combinations. These findings show multimodal perception does not guarantee robust reasoning; architectures must enable sustained cross-modal attention to avoid premature

</details>


### [122] [LC-GRPO: Bridging Train-Inference Gap for Flow-Based GRPO with Langevin Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05600)
*Yingqing Guo,Hui Yuan,Zijian He,Mengdi Wang,Zheng Ding*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出LC-GRPO，在流模型强化学习训练中，用ODE欧拉步加朗之万校正替代SDE rollout，以减小训练与测试推断间的分布偏差，提升奖励优化效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流模型推理用ODE，而GRPO训练用SDE；两者连续时间边际分布相同但离散化差异大，SDE rollout随噪声增大变模糊，导致训练样本与测试ODE采样不匹配。

Method: 每个rollout转移先执行与推理对齐的ODE欧拉步，再施加针对当前时刻边际分布的朗之万校正；分数由流速度直接导出，无需额外模型，转移保持各向同性高斯且似然可解。

Result: 理论上证明单步朗之万校正可降低不完美ODE欧拉步的Wasserstein误差，且在相同随机度下比标准EM离散化更准；实验在SD3.5-Medium、FLUX.1-Dev、HunyuanVideo上，LC-GRPO一致提升文本到图像和视频任务的奖励优化，保持生成质量，缩小训练随机rollout与测试确定性ODE推断的差距。

Conclusion: LC-GRPO是一种有效且实用的流模型GRPO改进，兼顾训练随机性与测试确定性的一致性，为扩散/流模型的RL训练提供了更优的采样范式。

Abstract: Flow-based generative models are typically sampled by solving a deterministic ordinary differential equation (ODE), whereas online reinforcement learning requires stochastic rollouts for policy exploration and optimization. Existing GRPO methods for flow models therefore replace the inference-time ODE with a stochastic differential equation (SDE) during training. Although the ODE and SDE share the same marginal distributions in continuous time, their finite-step discretizations can differ substantially. In particular, SDE rollouts often become blurry as the exploration noise increases, creating a mismatch between the samples used for reinforcement learning and those generated by the test-time ODE sampler. We introduce LC-GRPO, a flow-based GRPO framework with Langevin correction. Each rollout transition first takes an inference-aligned ODE Euler step and then applies a stochastic Langevin correction targeting the marginal distribution at the resulting timestep. The required score is recovered directly from the flow velocity, requiring no additional score model, while the resulting transition remains an isotropic Gaussian with a tractable likelihood for policy optimization. We theoretically show that, under suitable conditions, one Langevin correction step reduces the Wasserstein error of an imperfect ODE Euler step. At a matched randomness level, we further show that the proposed transition can be more accurate than the standard Euler--Maruyama discretization of the reverse SDE. Experiments on SD3.5-Medium, FLUX.1-Dev, and HunyuanVideo demonstrate that LC-GRPO consistently improves reward optimization across text-to-image and text-to-video tasks, preserves generation quality, and substantially narrows the gap between stochastic training rollouts and deterministic test-time ODE inference.

</details>


### [123] [Reasoning Errors Have a Region and a Direction in the Residual-Stream Trajectory of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05660)
*Hamed Damirchi,Ignacio Meza De la Jara,Damith Ranasinghe,Yuhang Liu,Javen Shi*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出三流检测器，结合轨迹位移与受限状态视图（向量量化的粗区域和归一化多层的细方向），在推理基准上选准确率提升最高12%，并泛化到事实类任务，表明推理有效性更适合从状态条件化的运动而非静态状态或去上下文化的轨迹中读取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于轨迹的方法只利用层间残差位移，忽略了更新前的状态；而恢复完整状态会引入可被捷径利用的信息，形成两难。

Method: 设计三流检测器：运动流（残差位移）+“粗区域”流（向量量化状态）+“细方向”流（归一化多层状态），三流互补，在不退化为全状态探测的前提下恢复状态上下文。

Result: 在未见过的推理基准上，选准确率较位移独立SOTA提升12%，较单层探测基线提升21%；在事实补全和事实验证上也超越所有对比检测器；消融显示运动、区域、方向信号互补。

Conclusion: 推理有效性的信号更适合从状态条件化的运动中获取，而非静态状态或去上下文的轨迹单独提供。

Abstract: As language models are increasingly used for tasks that require verifiable reasoning, reliably distinguishing sound reasoning from flawed reasoning has become an important practical problem. Recent trajectory-based methods seek this signal in layerwise residual-stream displacements, which capture how representations change while attenuating some stable, token-specific information. However, displacement omits the state from which an update originates, whereas restoring the full state risks reintroducing shortcut-prone information. We identify this trade-off and propose a three-stream detector that combines motion with two restricted views of location. A coarse region reader based on vector quantization and a fine direction reader over normalized multi-layer states. This design restores enough state context to interpret the motion without returning to full-state probing. On reasoning benchmarks unseen during training, our method improves selection accuracy by up to 12% over the displacement-only state of the art and 21% over single-layer probing baselines. Although trained only on reasoning benchmarks, it also reads factual completion and fact verification, ahead of every detector we compare against, which places the signal on correctness rather than on a kind of reasoning. Ablations further show that motion, region, and direction provide complementary signals. These results suggest that reasoning validity is better read from state-conditioned motion than from either static states or decontextualized trajectories alone.

</details>


### [124] [Negotiating Risk Boundaries in AI for Policing Through Mixed-Stakeholder Deliberation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05418)
*Mackenzie Jorgensen,Jo Reilly,Alex Sutherland,Miri Zilka*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 通过混合利益相关者研讨会，发现社区代表对警务AI总体开放，仅明确拒绝累犯风险评估等少数用例；将种族平等纳入考量并未限制讨论，反而引向工具有效性、实际收益及公平分配的普适性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 警务AI应用日益增多，种族偏见风险已知，但受影响社区代表极少参与决策，亟需探索包容性评估方法。

Method: 组织30名社区代表、警官和学者参与的混合利益相关者研讨会，针对13个警务AI用例进行风险与种族偏见焦点评估。

Result: 参与者多数支持AI采用，仅拒绝3个用例（尤其累犯风险评估，反对其前提而非实施）；种族公平优先未收窄讨论，反而促成对工具效能、普适收益与分配公平的综合考量。

Conclusion: 将种族平等视角纳入AI风险收益评估初期，可产生类似包容性设计‘路缘切割效应’的增益，建议在AI采纳决策中系统融入受影响社区代表。

Abstract: AI tools are being increasingly adopted in policing in the UK and worldwide. Racial bias is a known and well-documented risk, yet representatives of affected communities are rarely included in decisions about AI adoption. We present results from a mixed-stakeholder deliberation workshop bringing together 30 community representatives, police officers, and academics to assess the risks of 13 AI use cases in policing, with an explicit focus on racial bias. We found that participants were broadly open to AI adoption, rejecting only three use cases outright, most notably recidivism risk assessment, where objections targeted the premise rather than the implementation. Our analysis reveals that foregrounding racial equity did not narrow the deliberation. Instead, discussions gravitated toward a fundamental set of questions: does this tool actually work, will it deliver genuine benefit, and will that benefit extend to everyone? This integrated reasoning, reminiscent of the curb-cut effect in inclusive design, highlights the benefit of incorporating the racial bias lens into the risk-benefit analysis of AI use cases from the outset.

</details>


### [125] [When Does Consensus Mean Correctness? Measuring the Agreement-Accuracy Coupling with Semantics-Preserving Re-Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05670)
*Rasul Khanbayov,Hasan Kurban*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 通过科学图表程序化重渲染构建等价图像集RENDEQ，发现模型跨扰动一致性仅在误差分散度足够高时才能作为可靠性信号；基于自洽性微调反而损害准确性，与自然图像结论相反。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将模型在扰动输入上的一致性同时用作无标签可靠性信号和自训练目标，但其与正确性的关联缺乏直接验证，因为自然图像扰动难以保证语义等价且缺乏精确答案键。科学图表由程序从数据生成，重渲染可获得语义等价且答案精确的图像集，为量化该耦合提供理想实验环境。

Method: 构建RENDEQ生成器，对科学图表进行渲染等价重绘；在三个开放权重视觉语言模型上、每次发现均用三个独立实例验证；对比重渲染与重采样的准确性和可靠性，与平均token对数概率基线比较；分析鲁棒性差异来源的因素分解；并基于模型自身跨渲染一致性进行微调实验。

Result: 重渲染在准确性和可靠性上均优于重采样；一致性在三个模型中两个超过基线、一个持平，但此优势取决于渲染管线正确性；方差集中来源于绘图库风格因素，是第二大因素的2倍多、噪声底限的10倍；基于跨渲染共识的微调在五次重复中准确率全部下降，与自然图像上的正效应相反。

Conclusion: 一致性仅在模型错误分散度超过一定阈值时才能认证正确性；奖励一致性的目标会破坏这种分散度，从而消除一致性信号的有效性，解释了自训练在科学图表上失败而自然图像上成功的差异机制。

Abstract: A model's agreement across perturbed inputs is used both as a label-free reliability signal and as a self-training target, on the premise that agreement tracks correctness. That coupling is rarely measured directly: natural-image perturbations preserve meaning only by assumption, and no exact answer key localizes errors. Scientific figures remove both obstacles, a figure is drawn from data by a program, so redrawing it yields images that are semantically equivalent by construction and share a programmatically exact answer. We build RENDEQ, a generator of such render-equivalence sets, and measure the coupling on three open-weight VLMs, checking every finding across three independent instantiations. Re-rendering beats resampling on both accuracy and reliability. Agreement beats an evidence-carrying baseline, mean token log-probability, on two of three models and ties on the third, reversing an intermediate, buggy replication traced to a rendering-pipeline failure. The dispersion behind this is concentrated in one style factor, the plotting library, more than double the next-largest factor and an order of magnitude above the noise floor. Fine-tuning on the model's own cross-render consensus inverts: accuracy falls in every one of five replication runs, the opposite sign to published results on natural images. Agreement certifies correctness only above a threshold set by how diffuse a model's errors are, and an objective that rewards agreement destroys exactly that diffuseness.

</details>


### [126] [Stochasticity Is Not the Hard Part: Reduction and Complexity in Instructional Sequencing over Prerequisite DAGs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05455)
*Zonglin Han,Yichen Chen,Jiawen Jiang,Tongan Shi,Kristian A. Stevens*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文研究教学排序问题，将其建模为随机最短路径问题，证明随机性可被精确消除并转化为确定性最短路径问题，同时证明最优排序是NP难的（即使简化条件下），但在特定可实现的传递偏好和固定前置宽度下可多项式求解，并提出可计算诊断指标mΔ来评估排序优化价值。在真实课程数据上验证了“双重容易”场景，在构造实例上展示了困难场景及A*算法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解教学顺序如何影响学习效果，以及寻找最优教学顺序的计算代价，为教育实践提供理论指导和实用算法。

Method: 将教学排序建模为随机最短路径问题，通过状态依赖成功概率描述学习尝试；证明随机性可精确消除，转化为前置理想格上的确定性最短路径问题；通过归约证明NP难性；分析特定结构下的多项式可解性；提出诊断指标mΔ；在真实和构造数据上验证。

Result: 随机性可被消除；最优排序是NP难的（归约自锦标赛反馈弧集）；当传递偏好与前置关系联合无环时，任意拓扑序最优；固定前置宽度可多项式求解；mΔ可有效诊断优化价值；真实数据上显示低优化价值和小搜索空间，构造实例上显示贪心方法高遗憾而A*高效。

Conclusion: 教学排序的复杂性取决于问题结构：一般情况下NP难，但实际数据中可能容易，且可利用结构特征实现高效优化算法。

Abstract: When a student must learn concepts connected by prerequisite dependencies, when does the order of instruction matter, and what does it cost to find the best one? We study instructional sequencing as a stochastic shortest-path problem in which attempting a concept succeeds with a state-dependent probability and failure leaves the learner state unchanged. We first prove that this stochasticity can be eliminated exactly: the problem collapses to a deterministic shortest-path problem on the lattice of prerequisite order ideals, preserving optimal values and actions. The collapse removes stochastic complexity but not combinatorial complexity: optimal sequencing remains NP-hard -- via reduction from feedback arc set in tournaments -- even with no prerequisite edges, unit costs, uniform binary nonnegative transfer, and success probabilities at least $1/2$. Hardness is not uniform: when realizable transfer preferences remain jointly acyclic with the prerequisites, any topological order of the residual joint graph is optimal, and fixed prerequisite width yields polynomial-time exact dynamic programming. A computable diagnostic, $mΔ$, bounds the value of sequencing before optimization. On 70,893 interactions from an introductory CS course, the diagnostic certifies a doubly easy regime -- little value to optimize and little space to search -- while constructed transfer instances realize the challenging regime, where myopic sequencing suffers large regret yet exact A* with a consistent heuristic expands only linearly many states on that family.

</details>


### [127] [Innovation-Residual Auditing of Autonomous Analysis Agents: Localization, Detection Limits, Error Control, and Identifiability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05490)
*Ahmed Hassoon,Mark Dredze*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文首次系统分析了无监督数据分析审计方法的可靠性，揭示了评分函数选择对错误定位的根本影响，并提出了控制误报率、量化保证衰减及错误可检测性极限的完整理论框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主代理执行数据分析时缺乏逐步监督，错误定位依赖无标签审计方法，但其可靠性从未被系统研究。

Method: 通过理论分析不同评分函数（基于单步条件概率 vs 长窗口重建）对错误传播的影响，利用可交换性假设设计控制误报率的程序，并分析模型不完美及审查选择偏差下的保证退化。

Result: 单步评分使错误局限于单次操作，长窗口评分导致错误扩散；提出了控制误报比例的程序，量化了保证衰减，并证明错误检测极限随样本量增加下降极慢（百倍样本仅减少不到2%）。

Conclusion: 审计可靠性受表示维度而非训练数据量的根本约束，为实际部署提供了关键理论指导。

Abstract: Autonomous agents now carry out entire data analyses, selecting cohorts, joining tables, and fitting models with little step-by-step supervision. When such an analysis turns out to be wrong, someone must determine which operation caused it. A recent approach does this without any labelled mistakes, learning instead from analyses known to be sound and flagging operations that depart from what that model predicts; how reliable such audits are has not been studied. This paper supplies that analysis. The choice of score determines whether an error can be localized at all. If each operation is scored by how surprising it is given the operation immediately preceding it, then operations that merely inherit an earlier error are indistinguishable from correct ones, so one mistake produces one flag; scores computed against a longer reconstruction of the intended analysis instead spread a single mistake across many operations. We quantify how far they spread, and how to choose the comparison length when an error accumulates gradually rather than at once. We then give procedures that control the proportion of falsely flagged operations within a single audited analysis, requiring only that sound analyses be exchangeable rather than that the fitted model be correct, and we quantify how much the guarantees weaken when the model is imperfect or when the analysis was selected for review in a way that depends on its content. Finally we establish a limit on what any such audit can report: errors below a certain magnitude cannot be attributed at all, being indistinguishable from ordinary variation among sound analyses. This limit falls so slowly as more sound analyses are collected that at the representation sizes now in use a hundredfold increase reduces it by under two percent, so the dimension of the representation rather than the volume of training data is the binding constraint.

</details>


### [128] [EcoAgent-Bench: Evaluating Economic Decision-Making in Budget-Constrained LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05519)
*Jie Wu,Ming Gong,Feixiang Cheng,Qinqin Zhao*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出EcoAgent-Bench基准，用于评估智能体在预算约束下的资源选择能力，并引入经济一致性分数来揭示单纯追求任务完成率指标的缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准只关注任务完成度，忽略部署中资源选择（如查找、搜索、升级模型或人工介入）的重要性和成本，导致评估与真实场景脱节。

Method: 构建包含304个真实衍生任务的基准，每个任务带有定价动作和明确预算；覆盖四类决策（避免不必要升级、本地证据不足时升级、选择模型层级、基于不支持前提停止）；评估7个LLM智能体及4个oracle脚本控制，并设计经济一致性分数作为补充指标。

Result: 工具API智能体的微平均严格成功率仅为3.9%-24.0%，经济一致性最高仅7.3%；常升级策略在微平均上表现好但在节省任务上失败；预算扫描仅使GPT-5.4的升级率从0%提升到3%。

Conclusion: 预算内完成任务与经济地选择行动是两种不同属性，现有智能体在成本感知决策上严重不足，需要同时研究任务完成和资源经济性。

Abstract: Agent benchmarks usually measure task completion and treat resource use as an auxiliary statistic. In deployment, however, the choice among a local lookup, broad search, composite research tool, stronger model, or human escalation is part of the task itself. We introduce EcoAgent-Bench, in which every task specifies priced actions and an explicit budget. Its 304 real-derived tasks span five families adapted from GAIA, HotpotQA, and MuSiQue, and test four decisions: avoiding unnecessary escalation, escalating when local evidence is insufficient, selecting a model tier, and stopping on unsupported premises. We evaluate seven LLM agents in tool-API and workspace-CLI settings, together with four oracle scripted controls. Micro-averaged accuracy rewards one-sided policies: always-escalate controls achieve high micro success while failing save-oriented tasks. We therefore also report an economic-consistency score (the worse of accuracy on upgrade-oriented and save-oriented family groups) which exposes this failure. Tool-API agents attain only 3.9-24.0% micro strict success (at most 7.3% economic consistency), often either stopping before warranted escalation or overspending on cheap tasks. A threshold-crossing budget sweep changes GPT-5.4's escalation rate from 0% to only 3%. These results show that completion under a budget and economical action selection are distinct properties. We release the task bundle, transformation pipeline, frozen evaluation environments, and integrity-bound result artifacts needed to study both.

</details>


### [129] [CircuitSteer: Geometrically Aligned Multi-Layer Steering via Sparse Autoencoder Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05732)
*Mehrshad Saadatinia,Parsa Razmara,Ardalan Aryashad,Ali Abbasi,Seyedarmin Azizi*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出 CircuitSteer 框架，利用稀疏自编码器识别跨层语义电路，通过多点干预实现比静态单点干预更稳健有效的 LLM 行为控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法如 CAA 依赖固定单层干预，无法适应语义多样输入且跨层行为改变不一致，导致操控效果有限。

Method: 基于 SAE 构建特征流电路，通过特征共激活与解码器方向几何对齐筛选多层子电路，合成稠密操控向量并执行多点干预。

Result: 在毒性、情绪强度、谄媚和拒绝等任务及两个模型家族中，CircuitSteer 是唯一保持流畅性的方法，其他方法要么牺牲文本质量要么覆盖不足。

Conclusion: 多层电路操控辅以特征几何对齐比静态单点干预提供更稳健且更有效的行为控制。

Abstract: Controlling the behavior of large language models (LLMs) remains a critical challenge for AI alignment. Existing steering methods, such as Contrastive Activation Addition (CAA), typically rely on fixed single-layer interventions derived from aggregate activation differences. These methods impose a single intervention across semantically diverse inputs and often fail to sustain consistent behavioral changes across layers, limiting the effectiveness of the steering. In this work, we introduce CircuitSteer, a novel framework that leverages Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) to identify and manipulate coherent semantic circuits distributed across multiple layers. By constructing a feature flow circuit based on feature co-activation and the geometric alignment of decoder directions, we isolate the specific multi-layer subcircuits responsible for a target behavior. We then synthesize dense steering vectors from these sparse features and apply multi-point interventions to guide the model's internal semantic trajectory. We evaluate CircuitSteer using contrastive examples across a diverse set of tasks, including toxicity, emotion-intensity, sycophancy, and refusal, spanning two model families. Across all models and datasets, CircuitSteer is the only method to consistently produce fluency-preserving interventions; competing methods either sacrifice text quality or lack coverage, failing entirely on complex behaviors like sycophancy and refusal. These results demonstrate that multi-layer circuit steering, enabled by enforcing geometric alignment among selected features, yields strictly more robust and effective behavioral control than static single-point interventions. Code is available at https://github.com/mehrshad-sdtn/CircuitSteer.

</details>


### [130] [Hyper-ES: Effective Evolution Strategies for LLM Reasoning via Descent Direction Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05541)
*Yu Gu,Zhi Zheng,Yunpeng Ba,Xialiang Tong,Mingxuan Yuan,Zhenkun Wang*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出Hyper-ES，一种基于子空间的进化策略框架，通过少量梯度微调获取下降方向并构建紧凑子空间，再用CMA-ES优化DARE-TIES合并系数，在数学推理任务上超越GRPO-LoRA且减少10%的梯度更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接对十亿参数LLM应用ES低效，因高维参数空间中随机扰动与有效更新方向近乎正交，导致优化不稳定。

Method: 先执行少量廉价梯度微调获取下降方向，其张成紧凑适应子空间；在该子空间内用CMA-ES优化逐层DARE-TIES合并系数，搜索有意义的下降方向组合而非全模型随机扰动。

Result: 在三个Qwen2.5-Instruct和DeepSeek-R1-Distill骨干及六个数学推理数据集上，Hyper-ES一致超越GRPO-LoRA约1%，且所需空间消耗型梯度更新减少10%。

Conclusion: Hyper-ES证明结合少量梯度信息与子空间ES可有效提升LLM推理微调效率，为资源受限场景提供可行方案。

Abstract: Evolution Strategy (ES) is a promising alternative to gradient-based fine-tuning for resource-constrained Large Language Model (LLM) reasoning. However, directly applying ES to billion-parameter LLMs is highly ineffective. In such high-dimensional parameter spaces, most random perturbations are nearly orthogonal to useful update directions, leading to unstable optimization. We propose Hyper-ES, a subspace-based ES framework that avoids the weakness of ES in full-parameter search while exploiting its strength in low-dimensional optimization. Instead of asking ES to discover useful directions from random perturbations in the LLM parameter space, Hyper-ES first performs a small number of inexpensive gradient-based fine-tuning runs to obtain descent directions. Although each direction may provide only a limited improvement on its own, their span forms a compact adaptation subspace that captures useful reasoning updates. Hyper-ES then applies CMA-ES to optimize layer-wise DARE-TIES merging coefficients within this subspace, allowing ES to search over combinations of meaningful descent directions rather than over arbitrary full-model perturbations. We evaluate Hyper-ES on three Qwen2.5-Instruct and DeepSeek-R1-Distill backbones across six mathematical reasoning datasets. Results show that Hyper-ES consistently outperforms GRPO-LoRA by 1% while requiring 10% fewer space-consuming gradient updates. Code at https://github.com/kuangrepi/Hyper-ES.

</details>


### [131] [SkillTV-Bench: Benchmarking How Well Judges Perform on Skill-Augmented Agentic Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05573)
*Zhi Han,Chenxi Zeng,Liuhaichen Yang,Zihan Guo,Ming Zhou,Yang Li*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出SkillTV-Bench基准和SkillTV-Evolve方法，用于评估和提升智能体在长时程任务中对轨迹的技能感知验证能力，使裁判准确率提升14.8个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有裁判基准多关注最终回答或静态轨迹，缺乏结合任务时技能与可直接检查的工件和环境，无法有效评估技能增强智能体的轨迹验证。

Method: 构建681个真实轨迹案例的SkillTV-Bench基准，覆盖11个领域的50个任务；提出SkillTV-Evolve，将验证知识外化为可复用的JudgeSkill，并利用误判案例自动进化优化该技能。

Result: 优化后的JudgeSkill使同一裁判精度提高14.8个百分点；在离线rollout池选择中，10次rollout时选中轨迹成功率从22.9%提升至45.5%。

Conclusion: 技能感知的轨迹验证可通过外化与进化验证知识显著提升，验证方法在离线选择中也能带来实际收益。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly execute long-horizon tasks through tool use and environment interaction, shifting evaluation from final-response scoring to verification of complete executions. For skill-augmented agents, verification additionally requires the procedural knowledge encoded in task-time skills, because this knowledge indicates what evidence to inspect and which failures are task-critical. However, existing judge benchmarks often expose final responses or static trajectories, and rarely combine task-time skills with directly inspectable artifacts and environments. We therefore introduce SkillTV-Bench, a 681-case benchmark of real agent trajectories from 50 tasks across eleven domains, designed to evaluate skill-aware trajectory verification for both LLM-as-a-Judge and Agent-as-a-Judge methods. Additionally, we propose SkillTV-Evolve, which externalizes verification knowledge as a reusable JudgeSkill that guides an agent judge to plan targeted inspections and issue evidence-grounded verdicts. On a disjoint development pool, an automated evolution loop further refines the JudgeSkill using misjudged cases. On SkillTV-Bench, the refined skill increases the same agent judge's accuracy by 14.8 percentage points. In offline rollout-pool selection, it increases selected-trajectory success from 22.9% with one rollout to 45.5% with ten rollouts. The code and data are available at https://github.com/HanZhi306/SkillTV-Bench

</details>


### [132] [Equipment-centric workpiece localization in near real-time using deep learning-based vision and event-driven finite state machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05744)
*Dohyeon Kong,Jaebong Cho,Hyunbo Cho*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出一种基于设备观测的连续工件定位框架，通过多静态2D相机推断热锻工厂中的工件位置，结合关键点引导注意力机制与事件驱动有限状态机，实现高精度事件检测与可解释推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热锻过程中直接跟踪工件因极端温度、表面退化和不规则路径而不可靠，需替代方案实现连续定位以支持可追溯性和过程协调。

Method: 利用多静态2D相机观测处理设备，估计3D坐标并识别抓取/释放活动；通过事件驱动有限状态机验证活动为离散处理事件，持续更新工件状态；在3D CNN中集成关键点引导注意力机制聚焦功能相关设备区域。

Result: 在实际热锻工厂中实现100%事件检测准确率（33秒容差窗口内），平均定位误差317.8mm，平均系统延迟21秒。

Conclusion: 框架有效连接视觉感知与可解释事件推理，支持工件转移可视化及设备操作定量分析，为热锻连续定位提供可靠方案。

Abstract: Continuous workpiece localization is essential for traceability and process coordination in hot forging, but direct tracking is unreliable because of extreme temperatures, surface degradation, and irregular routing. This study presents an equipment-centric framework that infers workpiece locations from handling equipment observed by multiple static 2D cameras. The framework estimates floorplan-space 3D equipment coordinates and recognizes grasp and release activities. Event-driven finite state machines validate these activities as discrete handling events and continuously update workpiece states and locations. A keypoint-guided attention mechanism integrated into a 3D convolutional neural network improves activity recognition by focusing on functionally relevant equipment regions. Evaluation in an operational hot forging factory achieved 100\% event detection accuracy within a 33-second tolerance window, a mean localization error of 317.8 mm, and a mean system latency of 21 seconds. The framework connects vision-based perception with interpretable event-driven reasoning and supports visualization of workpiece transfers and quantitative analysis of equipment operations.

</details>


### [133] [StepReflect: Structured UI Transition Reflection for Mobile GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05587)
*Linqiang Guo,Wei Liu,Li Gu,Yang Wang,Tse-Hsun,Chen*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出StepReflect，将移动GUI智能体的逐步反思建模为基于显式状态转换和视觉证据的结构化预测，用8B模型达到82.16%的转换级准确率，性能超越GPT-5.2，并在多个智能体配置下提升任务成功率、降低成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有移动GUI智能体的动作反思依赖开放式的多模态推理，成本高且与GUI状态转换的结构化特征不匹配，难以支持长程任务。

Method: 采用三阶段训练流程：监督微调、师生蒸馏、偏好与奖励优化；将每步反思形式化为以状态转换规格和视觉信息为条件的结构化预测任务。

Result: 离线在AndroidWorld上转换级准确率82.16%，比零样本GPT-5.2高11.83个百分点；在线在四种智能体配置中三种任务成功率更高，一种差距不超过1个成功任务，且所有配置下API成本均低于GPT反思。

Conclusion: StepReflect是面向长程移动GUI智能体的实用、可本地部署的反思方案，可作为反复调用前沿模型反思的高效替代。

Abstract: Autonomous mobile GUI agents require accurate action reflection for reliable long-horizon execution. Existing approaches rely on open-ended multimodal reasoning after each action, which is costly and poorly matched to the structured nature of GUI state transitions. We propose StepReflect, which formulates per-step GUI reflection as supervised structured prediction conditioned on explicit transition specifications and paired visual evidence. StepReflect is trained through a staged pipeline combining supervised fine-tuning, teacher-student distillation, and preference- and reward-based refinement. Offline, the resulting 8B model achieves 82.16% transition-level accuracy on AndroidWorld, exceeding zero-shot GPT-5.2 by 11.83 percentage points under the same structured input. Online, across M3A, Agent-SAMA, MAI-UI-8B, and Seed-2.0-Pro, StepReflect achieves higher task success in three of four agent configurations and remains within one successful task of the GPT-5.2 Reflection Agent in the fourth. It also reduces paid API charges relative to GPT-based reflection in all four configurations. These results establish StepReflect as a practical, locally deployable alternative to repeated frontier-model reflection for long-horizon mobile GUI agents.

</details>


### [134] [Epistemic Trustworthiness in Generative AI: A Normative Framework for Warranted Reliance in High-Stakes Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05602)
*Nimisha Karnatak,Max Van Kleek,Nigel Shadbolt*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文提出生成式AI系统在专业场景中应满足认识论可信赖性三条件（认识论谦逊、认识论通达、抵抗认识论不公），以实现用户对输出的正当依赖，而非仅依赖正确性等标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI评估框架关注准确性、公平性、可解释性、安全性或用户信任，但未直接界定‘有根据的依赖’这一独立目标，即用户在何种条件下有理由将AI输出作为自身推理的输入；高风险的AI应用可能诱导行为性依赖而缺乏认识论合理性。

Method: 借鉴哲学中可信赖性作为能力与受众导向的论述，构建规范性框架，提出三个共同必要且不可互换的条件：认识论谦逊（系统表达能力局限）、认识论通达（允许用户检查、质疑、争辩）、抵抗认识论不公（承认用户为合法认识主体，不边缘化其知识经验），并通过法律、医疗、招聘案例进行分析。

Result: 案例分析表明，三项条件的缺失会产生标准准确性、公平性与可用性指标无法单独捕获的实质性伤害；这些条件既有区别又相互关联，任一单独条件不足以保证有根据的依赖。

Conclusion: AI系统设计与评估应转向以认识论上有根据的依赖为核心目标，而非仅以输出正确性为导向；需在开发与治理实践中整合三项条件，并据此提出设计及评估的启示。

Abstract: Generative AI systems are increasingly deployed in high-stakes professional contexts, where their outputs shape what users believe, how they reason, and what they treat as settled. This raises a central question for responsible AI: under what conditions is reliance on generative AI outputs epistemically warranted rather than behaviourally induced? Existing frameworks largely ask whether AI outputs are accurate, fair, explainable, safe, or trusted by users. These questions remain necessary, and each can contribute to warranted reliance. However, they do not directly specify warranted reliance as a distinct evaluative target: the conditions under which users are justified in treating AI outputs as inputs into their own reasoning. We argue that this requires an account of epistemic trustworthiness: what makes a system epistemically worthy of reliance. Drawing on philosophical accounts of trustworthiness as competence and audience-orientation, we develop a constitutive normative framework comprising three jointly necessary and non-fungible conditions. First, epistemic humility requires systems to represent and communicate the limits of their competence. Second, epistemic access requires systems to enable users to inspect, question, and contest outputs in context. Third, resistance to epistemic injustice requires systems to recognise users as legitimate epistemic agents and avoid marginalising their knowledge and experience. Through real-world case analyses in legal reasoning, medical reasoning, and hiring, we show how failures of epistemic humility, epistemic access, and resistance to epistemic injustice can produce consequential harms that standard measures of accuracy, fairness, and usability do not address on their own. We conclude by outlining design and evaluation implications for GenAI systems organised around epistemically warranted reliance rather than output correctness alone.

</details>


### [135] [Neuro-Symbolic Closed-Loop Control of Laser Powder Bed Fusion with an In-Loop Ontology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05773)
*Gisuk Hong,Jaebong Cho,Hyunbo Cho*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出一种几何条件化的神经符号闭环架构，将本体推理与统计学习结合，用于激光粉末床熔融过程中的过hang熔渣控制，在仿真中消除了缺陷并具备可迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 熔池深度等关键质量指标在制造过程中无法直接测量，现有几何盲控制器易产生过hang熔渣，需要一种能结合几何上下文、符号知识与统计模型的可解释控制架构。

Method: 构建符合标准的本体，在控制回路内用描述逻辑推理将过程目标与约束映射为可观测信号的参考值和边界；用高斯过程校准深度-宽度比及不确定性；基于几何上下文动态激活约束，并保持单个小规模二次规划求解。

Result: 在Eagar-Tsai替代模型上，相比几何盲控制器完全消除熔渣；双评分下仅有少量残余未熔合；对有意模型失配具有优雅退化能力；新材料与约束可通过修改本体数据实现重定向。

Conclusion: 架构在仿真层面验证了可行性，主要下一步是实验标定深度-宽度比，以推动实际应用。

Abstract: A geometry-conditioned, neuro-symbolic closed-loop architecture is proposed for laser powder bed fusion, in which a standards-aligned ontology operates inside the control loop and couples symbolic reasoning with statistical learning to set the targets of a constraint-aware predictive controller. The ontology links the process objectives and constraints to the signals a controller can observe, and a description-logic reasoner converts them into the references and bounds enforced on each scan. The demonstrated case is overhang dross, a quality limit on the melt pool depth, which governs quality yet cannot be measured during the build, is mapped through a geometry- and power-dependent depth-to-width ratio onto a bound on the observable width, with the ratio and its calibrated uncertainty supplied by a Gaussian process. The reasoner classifies each upcoming feature and selects the active constraints-adding a lack-of-fusion floor at overhangs, a monotone guard beyond the calibrated range, and an energy-density cap where a process window is declared while running only on changes of geometric context and otherwise leaving a single small quadratic program on the per-scan path. In an Eagar-Tsai surrogate calibrated to the NIST AM-Bench benchmark for IN625, the architecture eliminates the dross produced by a geometry-blind controller, holds dross at zero with only a small residual lack-of-fusion under dual scoring, degrades gracefully under deliberate plant mismatch, and retargets to new alloys and constraints by editing ontology data rather than code. The results establish architectural feasibility, experimental calibration of the ratio is the principal next step.

</details>


### [136] [SkillHEX: Improving Agent Skills via Hypothesis-Driven Autonomous Exploration and Exploitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05628)
*Yuru Feng,Yaoqi Chen,Beidi Zhao,Qianxi Zhang,Xinjiang Wang,Jianan Lu,Zhirui Wang,Shusen Xu,Zengzhong Li,Qi Chen*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出SkillHEX框架，通过假设驱动的自我验证与证据引导的树搜索，在有限交互预算下实现智能体技能的自主、按需进化，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技能维护依赖人工，成本高且不可扩展，而实际部署需要测试时的自主进化，但面临稀疏奖励和多种潜在失败原因混淆的挑战，贪婪式局部优化易陷入早期误诊导致的利用陷阱。

Method: SkillHEX采用闭环框架，将可证伪的失败假设转化为可执行测试，无需额外环境尝试即可产生诊断性证据作为密集奖励，并利用证据引导对持久技能修订分支的树搜索，动态平衡受支持编辑的利用与合理备选方案的探索。

Result: 在SkillsBench的87个任务上，使用GPT-5.3-Codex和Claude Opus 4.7在五次迭代预算下，SkillHEX分别达到55.9%和57.9%的平均通过率，优于现有自进化方法。

Conclusion: SkillHEX通过假设驱动验证与证据引导搜索，有效应对了测试时技能进化的稀疏奖励和因果混淆挑战，展示了在有限交互下实现可靠自主技能进化的潜力。

Abstract: Although agent skills equip LLMs with reusable procedural knowledge, manual maintenance suffers from high costs, unscalability, and misalignment. Real-world deployments thus require autonomous, on-demand skill evolution at test time, constrained by limited interaction budgets and a lack of training or validation sets. This setting introduces a severe sparse reward challenge, where outcomes conflate multiple latent failure causes. Under such ambiguity, existing methods that greedily refine a single incumbent skill are particularly vulnerable to an exploitation trap, allowing early misdiagnoses to exhaust limited trials along unproductive trajectories. To address this, we introduce SkillHEX, a closed-loop framework coupling hypothesis-driven self-verification with evidence-guided tree search. SkillHEX translates falsifiable failure hypotheses into executable tests, producing diagnostic evidence as dense reward without additional environment attempts. This evidence guides a search over persistent skill-revision branches, dynamically balancing the exploitation of supported edits with the exploration of plausible alternatives. Evaluated on 87 tasks from SkillsBench, SkillHEX outperforms existing self-evolving methods and achieves an average pass rate of 55.9% and 57.9% using GPT-5.3-Codex and Claude Opus 4.7 under a five-iteration budget, respectively.

</details>


### [137] [Learning to Rank Tensor Network Contraction Plans for GPU-Accelerated Quantum Circuit Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05819)
*Alfred M. Pastor,Maribel Castillo,Jose M. Badia*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出一个基于学习排序的框架，用于在GPU上高效选择张量网络收缩方案，通过结构特征和梯度提升排序器训练，显著优于随机和MinFill基线，并显示跨GPU的部分稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 张量网络收缩对量子电路经典模拟至关重要，但收缩方案的选择极大影响GPU上的实际性能；理论复杂度相似的方案在执行时差异巨大，而现有方法未充分考虑并行性、内存流量和收缩几何等执行因素。

Method: 将每个收缩方案表示为由配对收缩序列导出的结构特征，使用梯度提升排序器，分别以列表式和成对式目标在GPU测量数据上训练，并在分布内、电路族迁移和GPU后端迁移场景下评估。

Result: 学习排序器在多数情况下优于随机和MinFill基线，列表式模型整体决策质量最佳；跨GPU时排序基本稳定但非完全一致，源模型在第二设备上仍保持有效决策能力。

Conclusion: 学习排序是减少收缩方案搜索的实用方法，但性能仍部分依赖后端，提示未来需结合后端感知训练或跨设备泛化策略。

Abstract: Classical simulation remains essential for developing and validating quantum algorithms, but its cost grows rapidly with circuit size. Tensor-network contraction can reduce this cost by exploiting circuit structure, although its efficiency depends strongly on the chosen contraction plan. On GPUs, plans with similar theoretical complexity may perform very differently because execution also depends on parallelism, reduction structure, memory traffic, and contraction geometry. We present a learning-to-rank framework for selecting efficient contraction plans before executing them. Each plan is represented by structural features derived directly from its sequence of pairwise contractions, and gradient-boosted rankers are trained from GPU measurements using listwise and pairwise objectives. We evaluate the resulting models on diverse circuit families, using separate in-distribution and circuit-family-shift test sets, and compare them with random and MinFill-based baselines. The learned rankers generally identify better plans, with the listwise model providing the strongest overall decision quality. We also study backend shift by comparing empirical plan orderings on two GPU architectures and evaluating the source-trained models on the second device without retraining. The rankings remain substantially, though not perfectly, stable across GPUs, and the models retain useful decision quality. These results support Learning to Rank as a practical way to reduce contraction-plan search, while showing that performance remains partly backend dependent.

</details>


### [138] [Refining Over Resampling: Test-Time Self-Correction for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05643)
*Ahsan Bilal,Muhammad Ahmed Mohsin,Muhammad Umer,Lena Trigg,Ali Subhan,Muhammad Ali,Dean F. Hougen*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出一种无验证器的广度-深度细化框架，通过并行采样、自我批评与修正、多数投票，在多个推理基准上超越贪心解码、多数投票、验证器最优选择等方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时扩展计算虽能提升推理，但单纯增加采样易重复已有答案，而依赖验证器选择受奖励模型校准限制。

Method: 采样多个独立推理轨迹，对每个轨迹进行迭代自我批评与修正，最后通过多数投票聚合细化后的答案，广度保留多样性，深度修复局部错误。

Result: 在AIME24、AIME25、AMC、OlympiadBench和MATH500上，一致优于贪心解码、多数投票、验证器最佳选择、束搜索和前瞻解码；例如Qwen2.5-1.5B在MATH500上从最强验证器基线提升至58.0%，在AMC上从25.0%提升至32.5%。

Conclusion: 测试时计算用于细化采样轨迹比单纯增加采样或依赖验证器引导更有效。

Abstract: Test-time scaling improves LLM reasoning by using additional inference compute, but wider sampling alone can suffer from diminishing returns: new rollouts often repeat existing answer patterns instead of adding useful reasoning diversity. Verifier-based selection offers an alternative, but its performance depends on the calibration of an external reward model. We propose a verifier-free breadth--depth refinement framework that uses test-time compute to both explore and improve candidate solutions. The method samples multiple independent reasoning rollouts, refines each rollout through iterative self-critique and self-correction, and aggregates the refined answers by majority voting. Breadth preserves diverse initial attempts, while depth repairs local reasoning errors before aggregation. Across AIME24, AIME25, AMC, OlympiadBench, and MATH500, our method consistently improves over greedy decoding, majority voting, verifier-based best-of-$N$, beam search, and lookahead decoding across multiple open-weight models. For instance, with Qwen2.5-1.5B, accuracy increases from the strongest verifier-based baseline to $58.0\%$ on MATH500, and from $25.0\%$ to $32.5\%$ on AMC. These results show that test-time compute can be more effective when used to refine sampled trajectories rather than only to sample more candidates or rely on verifier-guided selection.

</details>


### [139] [Evidential Rule Learning for Interpretable Classification with Abstention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05859)
*Javier Fumanal-Idocin,Javier Andreu-Perez*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出FERL，一种将模糊规则学习与证据理论结合的快速可解释分类器，在单次确定性前向中内生提供置信度、不确定度和弃权能力，兼顾准确率、校准与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实部署中可解释模型不仅需准确，还需透明展示决策依据并在不确定时弃权；现有方法多依赖后处理校准或辅助模块，缺乏内生、高效且稳定的证据输出。

Method: FERL将模糊隶属度直接映射为证据信念/似然/弃权，单次前向完成，无需额外头部、留出集或重复推理；并理论证明其输出关于输入满足Lipschitz连续性。

Result: 在30个表格数据集上平均准确率比次优方法高2.6%；集合预测在效用折扣准确率（u65/u80=0.80/0.83）和覆盖率（0.92 vs ≤0.82）上优于朴素证据分类器；近OOD检测AUROC达77.7，与最强专用检测器相当；在概念瓶颈评估中距最强专用检测器仅2.3 AUROC，并取得最佳AUPR-Out和新型类别拒绝性能。

Conclusion: FERL以统一框架同时实现高精度、可信证据输出、稳定性和OOD/新型类别检测，且能指出异常属性，适合高可靠性可解释部署场景。

Abstract: Interpretable classification often requires more than accurate predictions for real-life deployment: models should be transparent about the evidence behind their decisions and abstain when they cannot decide reliably. We introduce Fast Evidential Rule Learning (FERL), a method that learns interpretable, accurate fuzzy rule models whose outputs are evidential. Unlike post-hoc calibration, FERL's belief, plausibility, and abstention capabilities arise directly from the fuzzy memberships in a single deterministic pass, with no auxiliary head, held-out set, or repeated inference. Our theoretical analysis further shows that FERL is Lipschitz stable, which means that its evidential outputs vary smoothly with the input. Against state-of-the-art rule learners, FERL is statistically significantly more accurate across a 30 tabular-dataset benchmark ($+2.6\%$ average accuracy over the second best). Its native set predictions attain the best utility-discounted accuracy among credal classifiers ($u_{65}/u_{80}=0.80/0.83$ vs.\ $0.79/0.80$ for the naive credal classifier), at higher set coverage ($0.92$ vs.\ $\le0.82$). FERL also matches dedicated out-of-distribution detectors on tabular near-OOD detection ($77.7$ vs.\ $77.4$ AUROC for the strongest baseline). Under detector-class-disjoint concept-bottleneck evaluation, its it is within $2.3$ AUROC points of the strongest dedicated detector on both CUB and AwA2, while attaining the best AwA2 AUPR-Out ($68.3$) and novel-class rejection ($57.2$), while being able to name which attributes are anomalous.

</details>


### [140] [A Unified Framework for Trajectory Prediction with Explicit Planning and Reaction Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05673)
*Jiaheng Chen,Jiaxing Li,Tinghe Zhang,Chaopeng Guo*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出INTRaJ，将轨迹预测中的社会影响分解为规划与反应两阶段，在四个基准上实现SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法未区分社会影响在轨迹规划中的功能角色，而智能体通常先预测他人未来行为再做局部反应调整。

Method: 两阶段分解：规划阶段利用未来社会信息构建参考轨迹，反应阶段从全上下文预测与参考的残差中恢复局部调整，支持多目标和单目标范式。

Result: 在Argoverse 2、Argoverse 2-ped、ETH/UCY和SDD上一致提升，尤其FDE和长时一致性，多个设置达SOTA。

Conclusion: 将轨迹预测重构为规划驱动的两阶段过程，验证分阶段社会建模对稳定预测的关键作用。

Abstract: Trajectory prediction has shifted toward structured formulations with explicit social modeling. However, existing methods inadequately distinguish the functional roles of social influence in trajectory planning. Observing that agents typically form motion plans by anticipating others' future behaviors before making local reactive adjustments, we identify social interactions as playing staged roles, namely planning precedes reaction. We propose INTraJ, a unified framework that decomposes social influence into two stages: a planning stage constructs reference trajectories using future social information, and a reaction stage recovers local adjustments from the residual between full-context prediction and the reference. INTraJ supports both multi-target and single-target paradigms. Extensive experiments on four standard benchmarks, including Argoverse 2, Argoverse 2-ped, ETH/UCY, and SDD, demonstrate consistent improvements, particularly in FDE and long-horizon consistency, with state-of-the-art performance achieved in several settings. INTraJ reframes trajectory prediction as a planning-driven two-stage process, validating that staged social modeling is critical for stable predictions. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/11isnotavailable/INTraJ.

</details>


### [141] [Grounded Well-Condition Anomaly Detection on the Volve Field: Constructed Labels, a Baseline, and a Dual-Head Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05685)
*Gospel Bassey,Vincent Fakiyesi*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 基于公开的Volve油田数据，构建了与工程文档校验的异常标签，并验证了无监督与有监督方法在跨井事件检测上的可行性与局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器状态监测基准多来自故障已知的测试台架，而真实生产环境缺乏故障日志，需自建标签；已有方法常忽视标签与物理故障的对应关系及可学习性验证。

Method: 利用Volve现场数据，依据工程文档构建带出处和推理的异常标签；采用无监督基线（一致性验证）和双头模型（同时预测事件发生与类型）进行可学习性测试。

Result: 无监督检测器与规则标签高度重合，证明标签非任意；监督模型在未见井上能较好恢复事件有无和类型，时间定位精度一般。

Conclusion: 公开了数据集、标签、佐证、基线与模型，强调诚实报告成功与失败，并明确所有假设，以推动可复现和可靠的现场异常检测研究。

Abstract: Most public benchmarks for machine-condition monitoring come from test rigs, where faults are induced on purpose and every event is known. Real production fields rarely offer that. They give you sensor histories with no fault log attached, which is exactly the situation where an anomaly-detection method has to invent its own labels, and where quiet assumptions can slip in unnoticed. We work with the open Volve field data released by Equinor and take two things seriously that such datasets usually skip. First, we build anomaly labels that are not just patterns in the numbers but are checked against what the field's own engineering documents say can physically go wrong, and we release the reasoning behind every label. Second, we test whether those constructed labels are learnable at all, using both an unsupervised baseline and a small dual-head model that marks when an event happens and what kind it is, an idea we carry over from earlier work on defect detection in metal parts. The results are honest. An unsupervised detector that never sees the labels still lands on the same regions our rules flagged, which tells us the labels are not arbitrary. A compact supervised model recovers event presence and event type well across wells it has never seen, and locates events in time only roughly. We report what worked, what did not, and every assumption in between. The dataset, grounded labels, per-label provenance, baseline scores, trained model, and code are released publicly under CC-BY-NC-SA 4.0.

</details>


### [142] [How Far Do Simple Transformations Translate Across Text Embedding Models?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05980)
*Sid Ali Hamideche,Louis Adrien Dufrene,Quentin Lampin,Guillaume Larue*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本研究调查了简单线性映射能否在不同文本嵌入模型间翻译表示，发现仅部分模型对兼容，整体不支持潜在普遍性假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证AI间潜在通信能力，即无需解码为人类可读文本，测试文献中关于嵌入空间潜在普遍性的假设在真实文本场景中的有效性。

Method: 对九种不同架构、池化策略和训练目标的嵌入模型，使用线性映射作为翻译器，通过CKA、下游传输、保真度和检索评估模型间兼容性。

Result: 简单映射能恢复部分共享结构并支持某些兼容对的传输，但对其他模型对则严重失败；兼容性取决于架构、训练目标、池化和数据分布。

Conclusion: 异构嵌入空间并非如文献普遍暗示的那样由简单映射普遍关联，潜在普遍性假设在现实中不成立。

Abstract: We investigate whether simple transformations can translate representations across heterogeneous text embedding models. Understanding how independently trained models organize semantic information is an enabler for AI-to-AI latent communication without decoding into human-readable text. Focusing on lightweight translators such as linear mappings, we test the literature hypothesis of latent universality in a realistic text setting beyond simplified benchmarks. Across nine embedding models differing in architecture, pooling strategy, and training objective, we evaluate compatibility using CKA, downstream transfer, fidelity, and retrieval. Simple translators recover meaningful shared structure and support transfer for some compatible pairs, but fail sharply for others. Compatibility depends jointly on architecture, training objective, pooling, and data distribution. Overall, the results show that heterogeneous embedding spaces are not universally related by simple mappings as often suggested in some literature.

</details>


### [143] [Unified Agent: Managing Interactions across Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05729)
*Xinshuang Liu,Runfa Blark Li,Shaoxiu Wei,Xin Lin,Truong Nguyen*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出跨设备、跨时间的智能体应维护紧凑且可操作的状态，并基于此构建Unified Agent，在基准测试中显著优于现有方法，且优势跨模型设置稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体系统在设计上未考虑跨设备、跨时间场景中观察分散的问题：单智能体将设备视为工具缺乏跨时间状态管理，多智能体系统协调但未维护紧凑的携带状态，导致性能不足。

Method: 主张智能体维护有效设计的状态，组织参与证据、陈述事实和待处理请求，形成紧凑、可操作的形式，以结合当前观察决定行动。基于该原则实现Unified Agent，并构建跨设备、跨时间的用户-智能体交互基准。

Result: 默认设置下，Unified Agent显著优于四种已发表设计的改编版本；在不同MLLM家族、能力和推理努力下，仍保持领先，表明状态设计优势具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 状态设计是跨设备、跨时间智能体性能的关键因素，Unified Agent验证了该原则的有效性，代码和数据将公开。

Abstract: As capabilities rapidly increase, AI agents can move from running inside one app to acting across a user's devices over time. Yet existing agent systems still fall short in this scenario. This is because observations are scattered across devices and moments, but mainstream systems are not designed around this fact: a single agent that treats devices as tools lacks effective state management for all devices across time, and multi-agent systems coordinate across agents but do not maintain the compact carried state a cross-device, cross-time request needs. We argue that the agent should maintain an effectively designed state that organizes engagement evidence, stated facts, and the standing request in a compact, action-ready form for deciding its action given the current observation. To compare state designs, we construct a benchmark of user-agent interaction across devices and time. We instantiate this principle in Unified Agent, a stateful agent that carries interaction evidence across devices and moments and uses it with the current observation to act. In the default setting, it significantly outperforms our adaptations of four published designs. Across changes in multimodal large language model (MLLM) family, capability, and reasoning effort, it remains ahead of all compared systems, demonstrating that the state-design advantage is robust across MLLM settings. Our code and data will be publicly available on GitHub.

</details>


### [144] [When Do Prompt-Side Agent Playbooks Transfer? Accuracy, Cost, and Runtime Shift in Agent Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05778)
*Weihong Lin,Lin Sun,Xiangzheng Zhang*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 冻结提示词剧本在跨环境迁移时仅条件性有效，需目标侧验证成本与终止条件，不能默认复用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提示词剧本虽能改善工具型语言体而不需重训，但其跨源环境的可移植性未知，需系统评估迁移协议与失败模式。

Method: 采用共享的蒸馏-验证-迁移协议，在ALFWorld、TAU2-Bench、XBench-DeepSearch三个基准上对比冻结迁移与目标侧再蒸馏，并执行全局多重假设校正与描述性异质性分析。

Result: ALFWorld中贪婪解码下迁移有益，蒸馏指导优于固定演示；TAU2-Bench平均优势微弱但全局校正后仅一个路径显著；XBench-DeepSearch中迁移产生重复查询、延迟终止与成本膨胀。

Conclusion: 冻结迁移是条件性的冷启动选项，而非默认复用策略；必须通过目标侧验证成功、终止、协议兼容性与成本，且未必优于再蒸馏。

Abstract: Prompt-side playbooks can improve tool-using language agents without retraining, but their portability beyond the source setting is unclear. We study frozen playbook transfer under a shared distill--validate--transfer protocol. On ALFWorld, transfer is beneficial under controlled greedy decoding and, in one near-budget-matched comparison, distilled guidance outperforms five fixed demonstrations. On TAU2-Bench, a prespecified aggregate contrast supports a modest average matched-domain advantage, but global Holm correction retains only one of 135 route-level effects; the remaining grid provides descriptive evidence of compatibility-sensitive heterogeneity. On XBench-DeepSearch, one artifact--runtime pairing preserves useful first-try heuristics while producing repeated queries, delayed stopping, and substantial cost inflation after a context-runtime shift. Across benchmarks, transferred and target-derived playbooks both require target-side validation of success, termination, protocol compatibility, and cost. Frozen transfer is therefore a conditional cold-start option, not a reuse-by-default strategy or a universally preferable alternative to target-side redistillation.

</details>


### [145] [Activity Frames: Deterministic Screen-Activity Compilation for Agent Memory and Replay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05784)
*Nossa Iyamu*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出一种零模型、确定性的屏幕活动编译管道，将被动捕获的用户屏幕记录压缩为代理可读的上下文块，比原始数据小86倍，同时保持高问答准确率，并首次测量代理成本模型中的例程开销比和例程重复率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机使用代理在推理时重复推导用户已完成的操作，因为现有代理记忆只记录用户所说而非所做，导致高昂的推理开销；而现有方法依赖模型总结，存在不可审计和成本高的问题。

Method: 采用确定性、零模型的管道：将本地捕获的屏幕流分割为类型化活动帧和边界化片段，携带应用、站点、时机、输入量和原始数据指针，不涉及模型，输出字节一致、可缓存、可机械审计，并作为需求侧成本工具提供两个模型参数。

Result: 在128,756帧的单用户语料上，一天原始捕获压缩为86倍更小的块耗时68毫秒，代理读取块后问答准确率98.4%，优于LLM总结的66-80%，且中档模型匹配前沿模型；首次报告R为60-343倍，h为9.0%样本内和7.7%样本外，全舰队token上限约8%，例程重放零token可演示。

Conclusion: 该编译管道既显著提升代理记忆效率和准确性，又为代理成本模型提供关键实测参数，且例程可确定性重放实现零token成本，系统已开源，验证了被动屏幕活动作为高效代理记忆来源的可行性。

Abstract: Computer-use agents pay full frontier inference to re-derive routines their user has already performed, because an agent's memory today records what the user said, not what the user did. We compile passively captured screen activity into agent memory with a deterministic, zero-model pipeline: it segments a local capture stream into typed activity frames, bounded episodes carrying application, site, timing, input volume, and evidence pointers back to the raw rows, with no model in the loop, so the output is byte-identical, cacheable, and mechanically auditable. On one professional's single-user corpus of 128,756 frames over 51 active days, the compiler reduces a day of raw capture to a prompt-ready context block 86x smaller in 68 ms, and an agent reading that block answers questions about the day at 98.4% accuracy (Wilson 95% CI 91.7-99.7%) against an independent oracle, versus 66-80% for an LLM summary of the same capture, a mid-tier model reading the block matching a frontier one.
  The same compiler doubles as a demand-side cost instrument. Read off passive, pre-delegation human activity rather than agent rollouts, it supplies two parameters that agent-cost models assume but, to our knowledge, have not measured: the Routine Overhead Ratio R and the routine recurrence h. We report first values of R, a modeled upper bound, at 60-343x, and a delegable recurrence of 9.0% in-sample and 7.7% out-of-sample, for a realistic all-fleet token ceiling near 8%; a compiled routine replays deterministically with the model out of the loop, demonstrated live at zero model tokens on a guard-matched hit. Schema, compiler, and evaluation harness are open.

</details>


### [146] [BioKD: Selective Physiology-to-Video Knowledge Distillation via Reliability Gate for Emotion Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06023)
*Bojing Hou,Ruohao Li,Yitong Zhu,Hongjun Liu,Luwen Yu,Yuyang Wang*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出BioKD框架，利用生理信号作为特权信息指导视频学生模型学习，推理时仅需视频输入，通过可靠性门控和渐进蒸馏策略抑制不可靠监督的负迁移，在DEAP和AMIGOS上优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频情绪识别在行为线索模糊或被掩盖时表现有限，而生理信号虽准确但部署困难，现有方法未充分处理生理教师监督中的高噪声和个体差异问题。

Method: 采用生理到视频的知识蒸馏，包含样本级可靠性感知门控机制和渐进蒸馏策略，动态调节知识迁移强度，避免不可靠生理监督导致负迁移。

Result: 在DEAP和AMIGOS数据集上，试用级和主体级评估中均优于基线，DEAP试用级唤醒度达68.01%，主体级达65.29%，且推理时无额外开销。

Conclusion: 明确建模监督可靠性优于熵值加权策略，有效缓解过度自信教师错误，可替代生理传感需求，提升视频模型泛化能力。

Abstract: To address the limitations of video-based emotion recognition under ambiguous or socially masked behavioral cues, as well as the poor deployability of physiological signals, this paper proposes a reliability-aware physiology-to-video knowledge distillation framework, termed BioKD. The proposed framework leverages physiological signals as privileged information during training to guide a video-based student model in learning deep affective representations, while relying solely on non-intrusive video inputs at inference time. To cope with the high noise and instability of physiological teacher supervision caused by inter-subject variability, signal artifacts, and temporal inconsistency, BioKD incorporates a sample-wise reliability-aware gating mechanism together with a progressive distillation strategy. By adaptively regulating the strength of knowledge transfer, the framework suppresses negative transfer induced by unreliable physiological supervision and enables more stable cross-modal distillation. Experiments on DEAP and AMIGOS show that BioKD consistently outperforms representative baselines under both trial-wise and subject-wise evaluation protocols for valence and arousal recognition. For example, BioKD achieves 68.01\% on DEAP (trial-wise arousal) and 65.29\% under the more challenging subject-wise setting, demonstrating improved performance under a subject-independent evaluation setting. Further analyses show that BioKD effectively mitigates overconfident teacher errors and outperforms an entropy-only weighting strategy, confirming the importance of explicitly modeling supervision reliability. In addition, BioKD introduces no additional inference-time overhead relative to the same video student architecture and removes the need for physiological sensing and multimodal synchronization.

</details>


### [147] [Hybrid-Adaptive Thread Tuning to Mitigate Simulation Execution Bottlenecks in High-Performance Reinforcement Learning Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06025)
*Jiming Su,Hantao Hua,Lujia Yin,Yiping Yao,Feng Zhu*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出AutoThread，一种混合自适应线程调优方法，通过PINO预测器和有限源M/M/1排队模型约束，动态调整线程数，缓解RL推理中的模拟器瓶颈，显著提升吞吐量和执行效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在仿真在环决策系统中，RL推理受模拟器执行开销限制，工作负载动态变化且对线程配置敏感。现有多线程策略难以在运行前或运行中匹配线程资源，导致资源争用、调度开销和吞吐下降。

Method: 识别任务执行时间与调度时间之比为最优线程数的关键因素；采用物理信息神经算子（PINO）作为线程数预测器，并结合有限源M/M/1排队模型进行约束和引导；通过负载感知的在线微调补偿预测误差并优化资源分配。

Result: 相比静态策略平均加速提升18.4%，平均吞吐量达XGBoost和Reinforcer的1.7倍和1.8倍，与最先进方法相比执行时间最多减少83.8%。

Conclusion: AutoThread能快速准确适应动态工作负载，有效缓解模拟器瓶颈，提升RL推理系统的整体性能，且代码和数据集已公开。

Abstract: In simulation-in-the-loop decision-making systems, reinforcement learning (RL) inference is often constrained by simulator-side execution overhead, where workloads are highly dynamic and sensitive to runtime thread configurations. Existing multithreaded strategies struggle to match thread resources before or during execution, causing resource contention, scheduling overhead, and reduced throughput. Through empirical analysis, we identify the ratio of task execution time to scheduling time as the key factor determining the optimal thread count. Building on this insight, we propose AutoThread, a hybrid adaptive thread-tuning method for mitigating simulation bottlenecks in RL inference. AutoThread employs a Physics-Informed Neural Operator (PINO) as a thread-count predictor and incorporates a finite-source M/M/1 queueing model to constrain and guide prediction, enabling fast and accurate estimation under dynamic workloads. It further performs load-aware online fine-tuning to compensate for prediction errors and refine resource allocation. Experiments show that AutoThread improves average speedup by 18.4\% over static strategies, achieves average throughput of 1.7x and 1.8x that of XGBoost and Reinforcer, respectively, and reduces execution time by up to 83.8\% compared with state-of-the-art methods. Our code and dataset are publicly available at https://github.com/suchenjm/AutoThread.

</details>


### [148] [Cautious Context Steering for Language Model Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05813)
*Gihoon Kim,Jeyoung Lee,Suhan Woo,Sekwon Oh,Minsu Jeon,Hyounsoo Han,Euntai Kim*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出了谨慎上下文引导（CCS），通过在冻结的骨干语言模型上添加轻量级适配器，逐token决定用户上下文的影响程度，无需针对用户微调即可提升个性化生成质量并降低推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个性化方法（如单独适配器或用户条件奖励模型）受限于稀疏数据且泛化差；ICL和上下文引导（CoS）虽无需训练但无法自适应调整上下文影响，CoS还需双前向推理。

Method: CCS在冻结的骨干LM上附加轻量级适配器，该适配器从oracle上下文条件LM中学习，在每个token生成时动态判断是否及如何利用用户上下文；当上下文无益时保持基础LM不变。

Result: 单一CCS适配器仅在一个数据集上训练，即可在域内及四个分布外个性化基准上提升生成质量，并优于ICL和CoS，同时避免了CoS的额外前向传递，大幅降低推理开销。

Conclusion: CCS以极低的训练和推理成本实现了对新用户和新领域的稳健泛化，为高效个性化语言建模提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Personalizing language models (LMs) to individual user preferences is essential for aligning responses with diverse goals and backgrounds. Existing methods typically train a separate adapter for each user or learn a reward model whose scores depend on the user. Despite explicitly optimizing for each user, these methods must learn from limited observations and therefore suffer from data sparsity and poor generalization to unseen users and domains. In-context learning (ICL) and Context Steering (CoS) can instead provide more effective personalization by conditioning the base LM directly on user context and leveraging its pretrained capabilities without per-user training. Yet neither adapts the influence of that context across decoding steps: ICL leaves it uncontrolled, whereas CoS applies a fixed steering coefficient and requires two LM forward passes per step. We propose Cautious Context Steering (CCS), which adds a lightweight adapter to a frozen backbone LM to decide at each token whether and how strongly user context should affect generation. The adapter learns this behavior from an oracle context-conditioned LM and preserves the base LM when the context is not helpful. A single CCS adapter trained on only one dataset improves generation quality both in-domain and across four out-of-distribution personalization benchmarks, demonstrating robust generalization to new users and domains. CCS also avoids per-user fine-tuning and the additional context-conditioned forward pass required by CoS, substantially reducing inference cost.

</details>


### [149] [ViSR-KGC: Visual Subgraph Reasoning with Vision-Language Models for Multimodal Knowledge Graph Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05833)
*Jiafan Li,Mengxue Yang,Jiaqi Zhu,Liang Chang,Ying Li,Hongan Wang*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出ViSR-KGC方法，通过将知识图子图可视化为图像并结合VLM进行多模态推理，以完成多模态知识图谱补全任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统嵌入方法在关系证据有限时表现不佳，LLM推理方法忽略图结构和视觉信息，而VLM无法直接理解图拓扑，因此需要一种能同时利用全局拓扑、局部多模态证据和常识知识的方法。

Method: 先学习多模态嵌入以捕获全局依赖，再提取查询感知的紧凑子图，将其按经验选择的布局策略可视化为图像，最后将子图图像、实体图像、文本描述和候选答案组合成统一提示，输入VLM进行推理。

Result: 方法整合了表示学习、VLM多模态分析和预训练模型中的常识知识，在多模态知识图谱补全上表现出有效性（原文未给出具体数值）。

Conclusion: ViSR-KGC通过视觉子图推理弥合了VLM与图结构化数据之间的鸿沟，为MMKGC提供了一种融合全局与局部信息的可行方案。
